Lemma 63
.

If

\eta\varpropto\epsilon
Then

1.

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x^{!}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

then

{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x^{!}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\epsilon{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}
.

2.

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}(\sigma^{j},\eta)\rightarrow\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

then

{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}(\sigma^{j},\epsilon)\rightarrow\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}
.

Proof.

1.

We consider the first case where if
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x^{!}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

then

{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x^{!}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\epsilon{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}
and by Def.
28
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}=\&_{\eta_{i}\in\eta}\{\mathtt{l}_{i};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta_{i}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}\}
. We now proceed by induction on the structure of
M
:

(a)

M={x}
.

By Fig.
8
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}{x}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}={x}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};[{x}\leftrightarrow u]
. We have the following derivation:

\mathtt{[(Tid)]}

[{x}\leftrightarrow u]\vdash{x}:\overline{A},u:A;{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x^{!}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T{\binampersand}^{x}_{d})]}

{x}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};[{x}\leftrightarrow u]\vdash{x}:{\binampersand}\overline{A},u:A;{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x^{!}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

For some type A. Notice the derivation is independent of
x^{!}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}
, hence holds when
M={x}
. Note that we do not consider
M={y}
where
y\not=x
, this is due to the case being trivial due to the typing of
y
being independent on
x
.

(b)

M={x}[ind]
.

By Fig.
8
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}{x}[ind]{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}=\overline{x^{!}}?({x_{i}}).{x}_{i}.l_{ind};[{x_{i}}\leftrightarrow u]
. We have the following derivation:

\mathtt{[(Tid)]}

[{x_{i}}\leftrightarrow u]\vdash u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x_{i}:\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta_{ind}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}};x^{!}:\&_{\eta_{i}\in\eta}\{\mathtt{l}_{i};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta_{i}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}\},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T\oplus_{i}]}

{x}_{i}.l_{ind};[{x_{i}}\leftrightarrow u]\vdash u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},{x}_{i}:\oplus_{\eta_{i}\in\eta}\{\mathtt{l}_{i};\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta_{i}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}}\};x^{!}:\oplus_{\eta_{i}\in\eta}\{\mathtt{l}_{i};\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta_{i}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}}\},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[Tcopy]}

\overline{x^{!}}?({x_{i}}).{x}_{i}.l_{ind};[{x_{i}}\leftrightarrow u]\vdash u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};x^{!}:\oplus_{\eta_{i}\in\eta}\{\mathtt{l}_{i};\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta_{i}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}}\},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

On the other hand we have derivation:

\mathtt{[(Tid)]}

[{x_{i}}\leftrightarrow u]\vdash u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x_{i}:\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\epsilon_{ind}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}};x^{!}:\&_{\epsilon_{i}\in\epsilon}\{\mathtt{l}_{i};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\epsilon_{i}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}\},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T\oplus_{i}]}

{x}_{i}.l_{ind};[{x_{i}}\leftrightarrow u]\vdash u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},{x}_{i}:\oplus_{\epsilon_{i}\in\epsilon}\{\mathtt{l}_{i};\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\epsilon_{i}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}}\};x^{!}:\oplus_{\epsilon_{i}\in\epsilon}\{\mathtt{l}_{i};\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\epsilon_{i}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}}\},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[Tcopy]}

\overline{x^{!}}?({x_{i}}).{x}_{i}.l_{ind};[{x_{i}}\leftrightarrow u]\vdash u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};x^{!}:\oplus_{\epsilon_{i}\in\epsilon}\{\mathtt{l}_{i};\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\epsilon_{i}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}}\},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

By
\eta\varpropto\epsilon
we have that
\epsilon_{ind}=\eta_{ind}
. Similarly for the case of
M={y}[ind]
with
y\not=x
we use the argument that the typing of
y
is independent on
x
.

(c)

M=M^{\prime}[{\widetilde{y}}\leftarrow{y}]
.

If
y=x
the case proceeds similarly to (
1a
) otherwise we proceed by induction on
M^{\prime}
.

(d)

M=\lambda x.(M^{\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}])
.

From
Def.
26
it follows that

{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\lambda x.M^{\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow x]{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}=u.\overline{\mathtt{some}};u(x).x.\overline{\mathtt{some}};x(x^{\ell}).x(x^{!}).x.\mathtt{close};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M^{\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}]{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}
.

We give the final derivation in parts. The first part we name
\Pi_{1}
derived by:

{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M^{\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}]{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\vdash u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x^{\ell}:\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma^{k}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\sigma,i)}};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x^{!}:\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}}

\mathtt{[T\bot]}

x.\mathtt{close};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M^{\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}]{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\vdash x{:}\bot,u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x^{\ell}:\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma^{k}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\sigma,i)}};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x^{!}:\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}}

\mathtt{[T?]}

x.\mathtt{close};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M^{\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}]{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\vdash x{:}\bot,u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x^{\ell}:\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma^{k}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\sigma,i)}},x^{!}:?\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T\mathbin{\bindnasrepma}]}

x(x^{!}).x.\mathtt{close};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M^{\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}]{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\vdash x:(?\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}})\mathbin{\bindnasrepma}(\bot),u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x^{\ell}:\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma^{k}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\sigma,i)}};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T\mathbin{\bindnasrepma}]}

x(x^{\ell}).x(x^{!}).x.\mathtt{close};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M^{\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}]{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\vdash x:\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma^{k}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\sigma,i)}}\mathbin{\bindnasrepma}((?\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}})\mathbin{\bindnasrepma}(\bot)),u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

We take
P=x(x^{\ell}).x(x^{!}).x.\mathtt{close};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M^{\prime}[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}]{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}
and continue the derivation:

\Pi_{1}

\vdots

P\vdash x:\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma^{k}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\sigma,i)}}\mathbin{\bindnasrepma}((?\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}})\mathbin{\bindnasrepma}(\bot)),u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T{\binampersand}_{d}^{x}]}

x.\overline{\mathtt{some}};P\vdash x:{\binampersand}(\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma^{k}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\sigma,i)}}\mathbin{\bindnasrepma}((?\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}})\mathbin{\bindnasrepma}(\bot))),u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T\mathbin{\bindnasrepma}]}

u(x).x.\overline{\mathtt{some}};P\vdash u:{\binampersand}(\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma^{k}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\sigma,i)}}\mathbin{\bindnasrepma}((?\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}})\mathbin{\bindnasrepma}(\bot)))\mathbin{\bindnasrepma}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T{\binampersand}_{d}^{x}]}

u.\overline{\mathtt{some}};u(x).x.\overline{\mathtt{some}};P\vdash u:{\binampersand}({\binampersand}(\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma^{k}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\sigma,i)}}\mathbin{\bindnasrepma}((?\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}})\mathbin{\bindnasrepma}(\bot)))\mathbin{\bindnasrepma}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}),{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

By Definition
28
we have that
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}(\sigma^{k},\eta)\rightarrow\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}={\binampersand}({\binampersand}(\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma^{k}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\sigma,i)}}\mathbin{\bindnasrepma}((?\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}})\mathbin{\bindnasrepma}(\bot)))\mathbin{\bindnasrepma}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket})
. In this case we must have that the variable names for
x
from our hypothesis and
x
from
M
must be distinct.

(e)

M=(M^{\prime}\ B)
, or
M=(M[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}])\langle\!\langle\raisebox{2.15277pt}{\small$B$}\!/\mbox{\small$x$}\rangle\!\rangle
, or
M=M^{\prime}\llfloor U/{x}\rrceil
.

The proof follows similarly to that of (
1b
).

(f)

M=M^{\prime}{\langle\!|}N/{x}{|\!\rangle}

Case follows by that of (
1a
) and applying induction hypothesis on
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}
.

(g)

When
M=\mathtt{fail}^{\widetilde{x}}

Case follows by that of (
1a
).

2.

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}(\sigma^{j},\eta)\rightarrow\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

then

{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}(\sigma^{j},\epsilon)\rightarrow\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}
follows from previous case along a similar argument.

∎

Theorem 64

(Type Preservation for
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\cdot{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}
)
.

Let
B
and
\mathbb{M}
be a bag and an expression in
u\widehat{\lambda}^{\lightning}_{\oplus}
, respectively.

1.

If
\Theta;\Gamma\models B:(\sigma^{k},\eta)

then

{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}B{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\models{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}(\sigma^{k},\eta){\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\sigma,i)};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}
.

2.

If
\Theta;\Gamma\models\mathbb{M}:\tau

then

{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\mathbb{M}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\models{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}
.

Proof.

The proof is by mutual induction on the typing derivation of
B
and
\mathbb{M}
, with an analysis for the last rule applied.
Recall that the encoding of types (
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}-{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}
) has been given in
Def.
28
.

1.

We have the following derivation where we take
B=C\star U
:

\Theta;\Gamma\models C:\sigma^{k}

\Theta;\cdot\models U:\eta

\mathtt{[FS{:}bag]}

\Theta;\Gamma\models C\star U:(\sigma^{k},\eta)

Our encoding gives:

{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C\star U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}=x.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(C)};\overline{x}(x^{\ell}).({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x^{\ell}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}\overline{x}(x^{!}).(!x^{!}(x_{i}).{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x.\overline{\mathtt{close}}))
.
In addition, the encoding of
(\sigma^{k},\eta)
is:

{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}(\sigma^{k},\eta){\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\sigma,i)}=\oplus(({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma^{k}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\sigma,i)})\otimes((!{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket})\otimes(\mathbf{1})))\quad\text{(for some $i\geq 0$ and strict type $\sigma$)}

And one can build the following type derivation (rules from Fig.
6
):

{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x^{\ell}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x^{\ell}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma^{k}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\sigma,i)};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}}\vdash x_{i}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T!]}

!x^{!}(x_{i}).{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}}\vdash x^{!}:!{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T\mathbf{1}]}

x.\overline{\mathtt{close}}\vdash x:\mathbf{1};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T\otimes]}

\overline{x}(x^{!}).(!x^{!}.(x_{i}).{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x.\overline{\mathtt{close}}\vdash x:(!{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket})\otimes(\mathbf{1});{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T\otimes]}

\overline{x}(x^{\ell}).({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x^{\ell}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}\overline{x}(x^{!}).(!x^{!}.(x_{i}).{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x.\overline{\mathtt{close}}))\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x:({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma^{k}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\sigma,i)})\otimes((!{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket})\otimes(\mathbf{1}));{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T\oplus^{x}_{\widetilde{w}}]}

x.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(C)};\overline{x}(x^{\ell}).({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x^{\ell}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}\overline{x}(x^{!}).(!x^{!}.(x_{i}).{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x.\overline{\mathtt{close}}))\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x{:}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}(\sigma^{k},\eta){\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\sigma,i)})

Hence true provided both
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x^{\ell}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x^{\ell}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma^{k}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\sigma,i)};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}}\vdash x_{i}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}
hold.

Let us consider the two cases:

(a)

For
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x^{\ell}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x^{\ell}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma^{k}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\sigma,i)};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}
to hold we must consider two cases on the shape of
C
:

i.

When
C=\mathtt{1}
we may type bags with the
\mathtt{[FS{:}\mathtt{1}^{\ell}]}
rule.

That is,

\mathtt{[FS{:}\mathtt{1}^{\ell}]}

\Theta;\text{-}\models\mathtt{1}:\omega

Our encoding gives:
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\mathtt{1}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x^{\ell}}=x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{\emptyset};x^{\ell}(y_{n}).(y_{n}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};y_{n}.\overline{\mathtt{close}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{\emptyset};x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{none}}).

and the encoding of
\omega
can be either:

A.

{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\omega{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\sigma,0)}=\overline{{\binampersand}((\oplus\bot)\otimes({\binampersand}\oplus\bot))}
; or

B.

{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\omega{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\sigma,i)}=\overline{{\binampersand}((\oplus\bot)\otimes({\binampersand}\oplus(({\binampersand}\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}})\mathbin{\bindnasrepma}(\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\omega{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\sigma,i-1)}}))))}

And one can build the following type derivation (rules from Fig.
6
):

\mathtt{[T\mathbf{1}]}

y_{n}.\overline{\mathtt{close}}\vdash y_{n}:\mathbf{1};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T{\binampersand}_{d}^{x}]}

y_{n}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};y_{n}.\overline{\mathtt{close}}\vdash y_{n}:{\binampersand}\mathbf{1};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T{\binampersand}^{x}]}

x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{none}}\vdash x^{\ell}:{\binampersand}A;{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T\oplus^{x}_{\widetilde{w}}]}

x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{\emptyset};x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{none}}\vdash x^{\ell}{:}\oplus{\binampersand}A;{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T\mid]}

(y_{n}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};y_{n}.\overline{\mathtt{close}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{\emptyset};x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{none}})\vdash y_{n}:{\binampersand}\mathbf{1},x^{\ell}{:}\oplus{\binampersand}A;{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T\mathbin{\bindnasrepma}]}

x^{\ell}(y_{n}).(y_{n}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};y_{n}.\overline{\mathtt{close}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{\emptyset};x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{none}})\vdash x^{\ell}:({\binampersand}\mathbf{1})\mathbin{\bindnasrepma}(\oplus{\binampersand}A);{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T\oplus^{x}_{\widetilde{w}}]}

x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{\emptyset};x^{\ell}(y_{n}).(y_{n}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};y_{n}.\overline{\mathtt{close}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{\emptyset};x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{none}})\vdash x^{\ell}{:}\oplus(({\binampersand}\mathbf{1})\mathbin{\bindnasrepma}(\oplus{\binampersand}A));{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

Since
A
is arbitrary, we can take
A=\mathtt{1}
for
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\omega{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\sigma,0)}
and
A=\overline{(({\binampersand}\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}})\mathbin{\bindnasrepma}(\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\omega{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\sigma,i-1)}}))}
for
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\omega{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\sigma,i)}
, in both cases, the result follows.

ii.

When
C=\lbag M\rbag\cdot C^{\prime}
we may type bags with the
\mathtt{[FS{:}bag^{\ell}]}
rule.

\Theta;\Gamma^{\prime}\models M:\sigma

\Theta;\Delta\models C^{\prime}:\sigma^{k}

\mathtt{[FS{:}bag^{\ell}]}

\Theta;\Gamma^{\prime},\Delta\models\lbag M\rbag\cdot C^{\prime}:\sigma^{k}

Where
\Gamma=\Gamma^{\prime},\Delta
.
To simplify the proof, we will consider
k=3
.

By IH we have

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}}

\displaystyle\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x_{i}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}\qquad{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x^{\ell}}

\displaystyle\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Delta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x^{\ell}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma\wedge\sigma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\tau,j)};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

By Def.
26
,

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\lbag M\rbag\cdot C^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x^{\ell}}=

\displaystyle x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(\lbag M\rbag\cdot C)};x^{\ell}(y_{i}).x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{y_{i},\mathsf{lfv}(\lbag M\rbag\cdot C)};x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};\overline{x^{\ell}}(x_{i}).

(11)

\displaystyle(x_{i}.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M)};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x^{\ell}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}y_{i}.\overline{\mathtt{none}})

Let
\Pi_{1}
be the derivation:

{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}}\;{\vdash}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x_{i}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T\oplus^{x}_{\widetilde{w}}]}

x_{i}.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M)};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x_{i}:\oplus{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T{\binampersand}^{x}]}

y_{i}.\overline{\mathtt{none}}\vdash y_{i}:{\binampersand}\mathbf{1};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}]}

\underbrace{x_{i}.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M)};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}y_{i}.\overline{\mathtt{none}}}_{P_{1}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x_{i}:\oplus{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},y_{i}:{\binampersand}\mathbf{1};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

Let
P_{1}=(x_{i}.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(M)};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}y_{i}.\overline{\mathtt{none}})
, in the the derivation
\Pi_{2}
below:

\Pi_{1}

{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x^{\ell}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Delta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x^{\ell}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma\wedge\sigma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\tau,j)};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T\otimes]}

\overline{x^{\ell}}(x_{i}).(P_{1}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x^{\ell}})\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Delta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},y_{i}:{\binampersand}\mathbf{1},x^{\ell}:(\oplus{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket})\otimes({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma\wedge\sigma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\tau,j)});{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T{\binampersand}_{d}^{x}]}

\underbrace{x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};\overline{x^{\ell}}(x_{i}).(P_{1}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x^{\ell}})}_{P_{2}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Delta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},y_{i}:{\binampersand}\mathbf{1},x^{\ell}:{\binampersand}((\oplus{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket})\otimes({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma\wedge\sigma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\tau,j)}));{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

Let
P_{2}=(x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};\overline{x^{\ell}}(x_{i}).(P_{1}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}A{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x^{\ell}}))
in the derivation below:

\Pi_{2}

\vdots

P_{2}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},y_{i}:{\binampersand}\mathbf{1},x^{\ell}:{\binampersand}((\oplus{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket})\otimes({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma\wedge\sigma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\tau,j)}));{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T\oplus^{x}_{\widetilde{w}}]}

x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{y_{i},\mathsf{lfv}(\lbag M\rbag\cdot C^{\prime})};P_{2}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},y_{i}:{\binampersand}\mathbf{1},x^{\ell}:\oplus{\binampersand}((\oplus{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket})\otimes({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma\wedge\sigma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\tau,j)}));{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T\mathbin{\bindnasrepma}]}

x^{\ell}(y_{i}).x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{y_{i},\mathsf{lfv}(\lbag M\rbag\cdot C^{\prime})};P_{2}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x^{\ell}:({\binampersand}\mathbf{1})\mathbin{\bindnasrepma}(\oplus{\binampersand}((\oplus{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket})\otimes({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma\wedge\sigma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\tau,j)})));{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T\oplus^{x}_{\widetilde{w}}]}

{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\lbag M\rbag\cdot C^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x^{\ell}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x^{\ell}:\oplus(({\binampersand}\mathbf{1})\mathbin{\bindnasrepma}(\oplus{\binampersand}((\oplus{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket})\otimes({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma\wedge\sigma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\tau,j)}))));{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

From Definitions
21
(duality) and
28
, we infer:

\displaystyle\oplus(({\binampersand}\mathbf{1})\mathbin{\bindnasrepma}(\oplus{\binampersand}((\oplus{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket})\otimes({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma\wedge\sigma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\tau,j)}))))

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma\wedge\sigma\wedge\sigma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\tau,j)}

Therefore,
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\lbag M\rbag\cdot C^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x^{\ell}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x^{\ell}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma\wedge\sigma\wedge\sigma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\tau,j)}
and the result follows.

(b)

For
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}}\vdash x_{i}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}
we consider
U
to be a binary concatenation of 2 components, one being an empty unrestricted bag and the other being
\lbag M\rbag^{!}
. Hence we take
U=\mathtt{1}^{!}\diamond\lbag M\rbag^{!}
with
\eta=\sigma_{1}\diamond\sigma_{2}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta_{i}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}=\&\{\mathtt{l}_{1};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma_{1}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},\mathtt{l}_{2};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma_{2}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}\}
by Def.
28
and finally by Def.
26
we have
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}}={x_{i}.\mathtt{case}\{\mathtt{l}_{1}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\mathtt{1}^{!}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}},\mathtt{l}_{2}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\lbag M\rbag^{!}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}}\}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\mathtt{1}^{!}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}}=x_{i}.\overline{\mathtt{none}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\lbag M\rbag^{!}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}}={\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}}
, we can conclude
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}}={x_{i}.\mathtt{case}\{\mathtt{l}_{1}:x_{i}.\overline{\mathtt{none}},\mathtt{l}_{2}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}}\}}
.

Hence we have:

\mathtt{[FS{:}bag^{!}]}

\Theta;\text{-}\models\mathtt{1}^{!}:\sigma_{1}

\Theta;\cdot\models M:\sigma_{2}

\mathtt{[FS{:}bag^{!}]}

\Theta;\cdot\models\lbag M\rbag^{!}:\sigma_{2}

\mathtt{[FS{:}\diamond-bag^{!}]}

\Theta;\cdot\models\mathtt{1}^{!}\diamond\lbag M\rbag^{!}:\sigma_{1}\diamond\sigma_{2}

By the induction hypothesis we have that
\Theta;\cdot\models M:\sigma
implies
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}}\models x_{i}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T{\binampersand}^{x}]}

x_{i}.\overline{\mathtt{none}}\vdash x_{i}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma_{1}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}}\vdash x_{i}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma_{2}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T{\binampersand}]}

{x_{i}.\mathtt{case}\{\mathtt{l}_{1}:x_{i}.\mathtt{none},\mathtt{l}_{2}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}}\}}\vdash x_{i}:\&\{\mathtt{l}_{1};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma_{1}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},\mathtt{l}_{2};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma_{2}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}\};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

Therefore,
{x_{i}.\mathtt{case}\{\mathtt{l}_{1}:x_{i}.\mathtt{none},\mathtt{l}_{2}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}}\}}\vdash x_{i}:\&\{\mathtt{l}_{1};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma_{1}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},\mathtt{l}_{2};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma_{2}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}\};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}
and the result follows.

2.

The proof of type preservation for expressions, relies on the analysis of twelve cases:

(a)

Rule
\mathtt{[FS{:}var^{\ell}]}
:

Then we have the following derivation:

\mathtt{[FS{:}var^{\ell}]}

\Theta;{x}:\tau\models{x}:\tau

By Def.
28
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}{x}:\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}={x}:{\binampersand}\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}}
, and by Fig.
8
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}{x}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}={x}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};[{x}\leftrightarrow u]
. The thesis holds thanks to the following derivation:

\mathtt{[(Tid)]}

[{x}\leftrightarrow u]\vdash{x}:\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}},u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T{\binampersand}^{x}_{d})]}

{x}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};[{x}\leftrightarrow u]\vdash{x}:{\binampersand}\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}},u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

(b)

Rule
\mathtt{[FS{:}var^{!}]}
:

Then we have the following derivation provided
\eta_{ind}=\tau
:

\mathtt{[FS{:}var^{\ell}]}

\Theta,x^{!}:\eta;{x}:\eta_{ind}\models{x}:\tau

\mathtt{[FS{:}var^{!}]}

\Theta,x^{!}:\eta;\text{-}\models{x}[ind]:\tau

By Def.
28
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta,x^{!}:\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}={\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x^{!}:\overline{\&_{\eta_{i}\in\eta}\{\mathtt{l}_{i};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta_{i}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}\}}
, and by Fig.
8
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}{x}[ind]{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}=\overline{x^{!}}?({x_{i}}).{x}_{i}.l_{ind};[{x_{i}}\leftrightarrow u]
. The thesis holds thanks to the following derivation:

\mathtt{[(Tid)]}

[{x_{i}}\leftrightarrow u]\vdash u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x_{i}:\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta_{ind}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}};x^{!}:\&_{\eta_{i}\in\eta}\{\mathtt{l}_{i};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta_{i}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}\},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T\oplus_{i}]}

{x}_{i}.l_{ind};[{x_{i}}\leftrightarrow u]\vdash u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},{x}_{i}:\oplus_{\eta_{i}\in\eta}\{\mathtt{l}_{i};\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta_{i}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}}\};x^{!}:\oplus_{\eta_{i}\in\eta}\{\mathtt{l}_{i};\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta_{i}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}}\},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[Tcopy]}

\overline{x^{!}}?({x_{i}}).{x}_{i}.l_{ind};[{x_{i}}\leftrightarrow u]\vdash u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};x^{!}:\oplus_{\eta_{i}\in\eta}\{\mathtt{l}_{i};\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta_{i}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}}\},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

(c)

Rule
\mathtt{[FS\!:\!weak]}
:

Then we have the following derivation:

\Theta;\Gamma\models M:\tau

\mathtt{[FS\!:\!weak]}

\Theta;\Gamma,{x}:\omega\models M[\leftarrow{x}]:\tau

By Def.
28
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma,{x}:\omega{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}={\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x^{\ell}:\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\omega{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{(\sigma,i_{1})}}
, and by Fig.
8
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M[\leftarrow{x}]{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}=x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{some}}.\overline{x^{\ell}}(y_{i}).(y_{i}.\mathtt{some}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(M)};y_{i}.\mathtt{close};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{none}})
.

By IH, we have
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}
.
The thesis holds thanks to the following derivation:

{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T\bot]}

y_{i}.\mathtt{close};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\vdash y_{i}{:}\bot,{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T\oplus^{x}_{\widetilde{w}}]}

y_{i}.\mathtt{some}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(M)};y_{i}.\mathtt{close};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\vdash y_{i}{:}\oplus\bot,{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T{\binampersand}^{x}]}

{x}.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(N)};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Delta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},{x}::\oplus{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[TCut]}

(\nu{x})({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}{x}.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(N)};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{{x}})\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Delta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

Observe that for the application of rule
\mathtt{[TCut]}
we used the fact that
\overline{\oplus{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}}={\binampersand}\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}}
. Therefore,
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\langle\!|}N/{x}{|\!\rangle}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Delta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}
and the result follows.

(i)

Rule
\mathtt{[FS{:}ex\text{-}sub^{!}]}
:

Then
\mathbb{M}=M\llfloor U/{x}\rrceil
and

\Theta,x^{!}:\eta;\Gamma\models M:\tau\quad\Theta;\text{-}\models U:\eta

\mathtt{[FS{:}ex\text{-}sub^{!}]}

\Theta;\Gamma\models M\llfloor U/{x}\rrceil:\tau

By IH we have both

\begin{array}[]{rl}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}}&\vdash x_{i}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}&\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};x^{!}:\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}\end{array}

From Definition
26
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M\llfloor U/{x}\rrceil{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}=(\nu x^{!})({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}!x^{!}.(x_{i}).{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}})
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};x^{!}:\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}},{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T?]}

{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},x^{!}:?\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}}\vdash x_{i}:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T!]}

!x^{!}.(x_{i}).{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}}\vdash x^{!}:!{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[TCut]}

(\nu x^{!})({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}!x^{!}.(x_{i}).{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}})\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

Observe that for the application of rule
\mathtt{[TCut]}
we used the fact that
\overline{!{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}}=?\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\eta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}}
. Therefore,
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M\llfloor U/{x}\rrceil{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Gamma{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket},u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}
and the result follows.

(j)

Rule
\mathtt{[FS:fail]}
:

Then
\mathbb{M}=\mathtt{fail}^{\widetilde{x}}
where
\widetilde{x}=x_{1},\cdots,x_{n}
and

\mathtt{dom}(\Gamma)=\widetilde{x}

\mathtt{[FS{:}fail]}

\Theta;\Gamma\models\mathtt{fail}^{\widetilde{x}}:\tau

From Definition
26
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\mathtt{fail}^{x_{1},\cdots,x_{n}}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}=u.\overline{\mathtt{none}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x_{1}.\overline{\mathtt{none}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}\cdots\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x_{k}.\overline{\mathtt{none}}
and

\mathtt{[T{\binampersand}^{u}]}

u.\overline{\mathtt{none}}\vdash u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T{\binampersand}^{x_{1}}]}

x_{1}.\overline{\mathtt{none}}\vdash_{1}:{\binampersand}\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma_{1}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T{\binampersand}^{x_{n}}]}

x_{n}.\overline{\mathtt{none}}\vdash x_{n}:{\binampersand}\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma_{n}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\vdots

x_{1}.\overline{\mathtt{none}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}\cdots\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x_{k}.\overline{\mathtt{none}}\vdash x_{1}:{\binampersand}\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma_{1}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}},\cdots,x_{n}:{\binampersand}\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma_{n}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

\mathtt{[T\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}]}

u.\overline{\mathtt{none}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x_{1}.\overline{\mathtt{none}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}\cdots\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x_{k}.\overline{\mathtt{none}}\vdash x_{1}:{\binampersand}\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma_{1}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}},\cdots,x_{n}:{\binampersand}\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma_{n}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}},u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}

Thus,
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\mathtt{fail}^{x_{1},\cdots,x_{n}}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\vdash x_{1}:{\binampersand}\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma_{1}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}},\cdots,x_{n}:{\binampersand}\overline{{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\sigma_{n}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}},u:{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\tau{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\Theta{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}
and the result follows.

(k)

Rule
\mathtt{[FS:sum]}
:
This case follows easily by IH.

∎

F.2
Operational Correspondence: Completeness and Soundness

Proposition 65
.

Let
N
be a well-formed linearly closed
u\widehat{\lambda}^{\lightning}_{\oplus}
-term with
\mathsf{head}(N)=x
(
x
denoting either linear or unrestricted occurrence of
x
) such that
\mathsf{lfv}(N)=\emptyset
and
N
does not fail, that is, there is no
Q\in u\widehat{\lambda}^{\lightning}_{\oplus}
for which there is a reduction
N\longrightarrow_{\mathtt{[RS:Fail]}}Q
.
Then,

{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\longrightarrow^{*}\bigoplus_{i\in I}(\nu\widetilde{y})({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}x{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{n}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}P_{i})

for some index set
I
, names
\widetilde{y}
and
n
, and processes
P_{i}
.

Proof.

By induction on the structure of
N
.

1.

N={x}
or
N=x[j]
:

These cases are trivial, and follow taking

I=\emptyset
and
\widetilde{y}=\emptyset
.

2.

N=(M\ B)
:

Then
\mathsf{head}(M\ B)=\mathsf{head}(M)=x
then

{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}={\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M\ B{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}=\bigoplus_{B_{i}\in\mathsf{PER}(B)}(\nu v)({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{v}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}v.\mathtt{some}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(B)};\overline{v}(x).([v\leftrightarrow u]\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}B^{x}_{i}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}))

and the proof follows by induction on
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}
.

3.

N=(M[\widetilde{y}\leftarrow y])\langle\!\langle\raisebox{2.15277pt}{\small$C\star U$}\!/\mbox{\small$y$}\rangle\!\rangle
:

Then
\mathsf{head}((M[\widetilde{y}\leftarrow y])\langle\!\langle\raisebox{2.15277pt}{\small$C\star U$}\!/\mbox{\small$y$}\rangle\!\rangle)=\mathsf{head}((M[\widetilde{y}\leftarrow y]))=x
. As
N\longrightarrow_{\mathtt{[R]}}
where
\mathtt{[R]}\not=\mathtt{[RS:Fail]}
we must have that
\mathsf{size}(\widetilde{y})=\mathsf{size}(C)
. Thus,

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}(M[\widetilde{y}\leftarrow y])\langle\!\langle\raisebox{2.15277pt}{\small$C\star U$}\!/\mbox{\small$y$}\rangle\!\rangle{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}

\displaystyle=\bigoplus_{C_{i}\in\mathsf{PER}(C)}(\nu y)(y.\overline{\mathtt{some}};y(y^{\ell}).y(y^{!}).y.\mathtt{close};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M[\widetilde{y}\leftarrow{y}]{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C_{i}\star U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{y})

\displaystyle=\bigoplus_{C_{i}\in\mathsf{PER}(C)}(\nu y)(y.\overline{\mathtt{some}};y(y^{\ell}).y(y^{!}).y.\mathtt{close};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M[\widetilde{y}\leftarrow{y}]{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}

\displaystyle y.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(C)};\overline{y}(y^{\ell}).({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C_{i}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{y^{\ell}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}\overline{y}(y^{!}).(!y^{!}.(y_{i}).{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{y_{i}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}y.\overline{\mathtt{close}})))

\displaystyle\longrightarrow^{*}\bigoplus_{C_{i}\in\mathsf{PER}(C)}(\nu y^{\ell},y^{!})({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M[\widetilde{y}\leftarrow y^{\ell}]{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C_{i}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{y^{\ell}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}!y^{!}.(y_{i}).{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{y_{i}})

\displaystyle=\bigoplus_{C_{i}\in\mathsf{PER}(C)}(\nu y^{\ell},y^{!})(y^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{some}}.\overline{y^{\ell}}(z_{1}).(z_{1}.\mathtt{some}_{\emptyset};z_{1}.\mathtt{close};0\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}y^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};

\displaystyle y^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{u,(\mathsf{lfv}(M)\setminus{y}_{1},\cdots,{y}_{n})};y^{\ell}({y}_{1}).\cdots y^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{some}}.\overline{y^{\ell}}(z_{n}).(z_{n}.\mathtt{some}_{\emptyset};z_{n}.\mathtt{close};0

\displaystyle\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}y^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};y^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{u,(\mathsf{lfv}(M)\setminus{y}_{n})};y^{\ell}({y}_{n}).y^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};\overline{y^{\ell}}(z_{n+1}).(z_{n+1}.\mathtt{some}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(M)};

\displaystyle z_{n+1}.\mathtt{close};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}y^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{none}}))\cdots)\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}y^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(C)};y^{\ell}(z_{1}).y^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{z_{1},\mathsf{lfv}(C)};y^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};

\displaystyle\overline{y^{\ell}}({y}_{1}).({y}_{1}.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(C_{i}(1))};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C_{i}(1){\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{{y}_{1}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}\cdots y^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(C_{i}(n))};y^{\ell}(z_{n}).y^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{z_{n},\mathsf{lfv}(C_{i}(n))};

\displaystyle y^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};\overline{y^{\ell}}({y}_{n}).({y}_{n}.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(C_{i}(n))};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C_{i}(n){\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{{y}_{n}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}y^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{\emptyset};y^{\ell}(z_{n+1}).(z_{n+1}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};

\displaystyle z_{n+1}.\overline{\mathtt{close}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}y^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{\emptyset};y^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{none}})\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}z_{1}.\overline{\mathtt{none}})\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}\cdots\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}z_{n}.\overline{\mathtt{none}})\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}!y^{!}.(y_{i}).{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{y_{i}})

\displaystyle\longrightarrow^{*}\bigoplus_{C_{i}\in\mathsf{PER}(C)}(\nu\widetilde{y},y^{!})({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}{y}_{1}.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(C_{i}(1))};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C_{i}(1){\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{{y}_{1}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}\cdots\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}{y}_{n}.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(C_{i}(n))};

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}{\displaystyle\llbracket}C_{i}(n){\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{{y}_{n}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}!y^{!}.(y_{i}).{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{y_{i}})

and the result follows by induction on
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}
.

4.

N=M{\langle\!|}N^{\prime}/{y}{|\!\rangle}
and
N=M\llfloor u/{y}\rrceil
:

These cases follow easily by induction on
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}
.

∎

F.2.1
Completeness

Here again, because of the diamond property (Proposition
33
), it suffices to consider a completeness result based on a single reduction step in
u\widehat{\lambda}^{\lightning}_{\oplus}
:

Notation 8
.

We use the notation
\mathsf{lfv}(M).\overline{\mathtt{none}}
and
\widetilde{x}.\overline{\mathtt{none}}
where
\mathsf{lfv}(M)
or
\widetilde{x}
are equal to
x_{1},\cdots,x_{k}
to describe a process of the form
x_{1}.\overline{\mathtt{none}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}\cdots\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x_{k}.\overline{\mathtt{none}}

Theorem 66

(Well Formed Operational Completeness)
.

Let
\mathbb{N}
and
\mathbb{M}
be well-formed, linearly closed
u\widehat{\lambda}^{\lightning}_{\oplus}
expressions. If
\mathbb{N}\longrightarrow\mathbb{M}
then there exists
Q
such that
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\mathbb{N}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\longrightarrow^{*}Q\equiv{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\mathbb{M}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}
.

Proof.

By induction on the reduction rule applied to infer
\mathbb{N}\longrightarrow\mathbb{M}
.
We have ten cases.

1.

Case
\mathtt{[RS:Beta]}
:

Then
\mathbb{N}=(\lambda x.(M[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}]))B\longrightarrow(M[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}])\langle\!\langle\raisebox{2.15277pt}{\small$B$}\!/\mbox{\small$x$}\rangle\!\rangle=\mathbb{M}
, where
B=C\star U
. Notice that

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\mathbb{N}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}=

\displaystyle\bigoplus_{C_{i}\in\mathsf{PER}(C)}(\nu v)({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\lambda x.(M[{\widetilde{x}}\leftarrow{x}]){\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{v}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}v.\mathtt{some}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(C)};\overline{v}(x).([v\leftrightarrow u]\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C_{i}\star U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigoplus_{C_{i}\in\mathsf{PER}(C)}(\nu v)(v.\overline{\mathtt{some}};v(x).x.\overline{\mathtt{some}};x(x^{\ell}).x(x^{!}).x.\mathtt{close};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M[\widetilde{x}\leftarrow x]{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{v}

\displaystyle\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}v.\mathtt{some}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(C)};\overline{v}(x).({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C_{i}\star U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}[v\leftrightarrow u]))

\displaystyle\longrightarrow

\displaystyle\bigoplus_{C_{i}\in\mathsf{PER}(C)}(\nu v)(v(x).x.\overline{\mathtt{some}};x(x^{\ell}).x(x^{!}).x.\mathtt{close};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M[\widetilde{x}\leftarrow x]{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{v}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}\overline{v}(x).({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C_{i}\star U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x}

\displaystyle\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}[v\leftrightarrow u]))

\displaystyle\longrightarrow

\displaystyle\bigoplus_{C_{i}\in\mathsf{PER}(C)}(\nu v,x)(x.\overline{\mathtt{some}};x(x^{\ell}).x(x^{!}).x.\mathtt{close};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M[\widetilde{x}\leftarrow x]{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{v}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C_{i}\star U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}[v\leftrightarrow u])

\displaystyle\longrightarrow

\displaystyle\bigoplus_{C_{i}\in\mathsf{PER}(C)}(\nu x)(x.\overline{\mathtt{some}};x(x^{\ell}).x(x^{!}).x.\mathtt{close};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M[\widetilde{x}\leftarrow x]{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{v}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C_{i}\star U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x})={\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\mathbb{M}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}

and the result follows.

2.

Case
\mathtt{[RS:Ex\text{-}Sub]}
:

Then
\mathbb{N}=M[{x}_{1},\!\cdots\!,{x}_{k}\leftarrow{x}]\langle\!\langle\raisebox{2.15277pt}{\small$C\star U$}\!/\mbox{\small$x$}\rangle\!\rangle
, with
C=\lbag M_{1}\rbag\cdots\lbag M_{k}\rbag
,
k\geq 0
and
M\not=\mathtt{fail}^{\widetilde{y}}
.

The reduction is

\mathbb{N}=M[{x}_{1},\!\cdots\!,{x}_{k}\leftarrow{x}]\langle\!\langle\raisebox{2.15277pt}{\small$C\star U$}\!/\mbox{\small$x$}\rangle\!\rangle\longrightarrow\sum_{C_{i}\in\mathsf{PER}(C)}M{\langle\!|}C_{i}(1)/{x_{1}}{|\!\rangle}\cdots{\langle\!|}C_{i}(k)/{x_{k}}{|\!\rangle}\llfloor U/{x}\rrceil=\mathbb{M}.

We detail the encodings of
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\mathbb{N}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\mathbb{M}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}
. To simplify the proof, we will consider
k=1
(the case in which
k>1
is follows analogously, similarly the case of
k=0
is contained within the proof of
k=1
).

On the one hand, we have:

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\mathbb{N}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M[{x}_{1}\leftarrow{x}]\langle\!\langle\raisebox{2.15277pt}{\small$C\star U$}\!/\mbox{\small$x$}\rangle\!\rangle{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}

\displaystyle=\bigoplus_{C_{i}\in\mathsf{PER}(C)}(\nu x)(x.\overline{\mathtt{some}};x(x^{\ell}).x(x^{!}).x.\mathtt{close};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M[{x}_{1}\leftarrow{x}]{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C_{i}\star U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x})

\displaystyle=\bigoplus_{C_{i}\in\mathsf{PER}(C)}(\nu x)(x.\overline{\mathtt{some}};x(x^{\ell}).x(x^{!}).x.\mathtt{close};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M[{x}_{1}\leftarrow{x}]{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(C)};\overline{x}(x^{\ell}).

\displaystyle({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C_{i}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x^{\ell}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}\overline{x}(x^{!}).(!x^{!}.(x_{i}).{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x.\overline{\mathtt{close}})))\qquad(:=P_{\mathbb{N}})

Note that

\displaystyle P_{\mathbb{N}}\longrightarrow^{*}

\displaystyle\bigoplus_{C_{i}\in\mathsf{PER}(C)}(\nu x^{\ell},x^{!})({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M[{x}_{1}\leftarrow{x}]{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C_{i}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x^{\ell}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}!x^{!}.(x_{i}).{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigoplus_{C_{i}\in\mathsf{PER}(C)}(\nu x^{\ell},x^{!})(x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{some}}.\overline{x^{\ell}}(y_{1}).(y_{1}.\mathtt{some}_{\emptyset};y_{1}.\mathtt{close};0\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{u,(\mathsf{lfv}(M)\setminus{x}_{1})};

\displaystyle x^{\ell}({x}_{1}).x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};\overline{x^{\ell}}(y_{2}).(y_{2}.\mathtt{some}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(M)};y_{2}.\mathtt{close};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{none}}))\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(B_{i}(1))};

\displaystyle x^{\ell}(y_{1}).x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{y_{1},\mathsf{lfv}(C_{i}(1))};x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};\overline{x^{\ell}}({x}_{1}).({x}_{1}.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(C_{i}(1))};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C_{i}(1){\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{{x}_{1}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}y_{1}.\overline{\mathtt{none}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x^{\ell}.

\displaystyle\mathtt{some}_{\emptyset};x^{\ell}(y_{2}).(y_{2}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};y_{2}.\overline{\mathtt{close}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{\emptyset};x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{none}}))\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}!x^{!}.(x_{i}).{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}})

\displaystyle\longrightarrow^{*}\bigoplus_{C_{i}\in\mathsf{PER}(C)}(\nu{x}_{1},x^{!})({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}{x}_{1}.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(C_{i}(1))};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C_{i}(1){\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{{x}_{1}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}!x^{!}.(x_{i}).{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}})={\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\mathbb{M}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}

and the result follows.

\mathsf{head}(M)={x}
and
\mathbb{N}\longrightarrow M{\{\!|}N/{x}{|\!\}}=\mathbb{M}
.

\displaystyle={\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\langle\!|}N/{x}{|\!\rangle}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}

\displaystyle=(\nu{x})({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}{x}.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(N)};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{{x}})

\displaystyle\longrightarrow^{*}(\nu{x})(\bigoplus_{i\in I}(\nu\widetilde{y})({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}{x}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{j}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}P_{i})\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}{x}.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(N)};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{{x}})\qquad(*)

\displaystyle=(\nu{x})(\bigoplus_{i\in I}(\nu\widetilde{y})({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}{x}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{j}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}P_{i})\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}{x}.\mathtt{some};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{{x}})

\displaystyle\longrightarrow(\nu{x})(\bigoplus_{i\in I}(\nu\widetilde{y})([{x}\leftrightarrow j]\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}P_{i})\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{{x}})

where the reductions denoted by
(*)
are inferred via Proposition
65
, and the result follows.

6.

Case
\mathtt{[RS{:}Fail^{\ell}]}
:

Then,

\mathbb{N}=M[{x}_{1},\!\cdots\!,{x}_{k}\leftarrow{x}]\ \langle\!\langle\raisebox{2.15277pt}{\small$C\star U$}\!/\mbox{\small$x$}\rangle\!\rangle
with
k\neq\mathsf{size}(C)
and

\mathbb{N}\longrightarrow\sum_{C_{i}\in\mathsf{PER}(C)}\mathtt{fail}^{\widetilde{y}}=\mathbb{M}
, where
\widetilde{y}=(\mathsf{lfv}(M)\setminus\{{x}_{1},\cdots,{x}_{k}\})\cup\mathsf{lfv}(C)
.

Let
\mathsf{size}(C)=l
and we assume that
k>l
(proceed similarly for
k>l
). Hence
k=l+m
for some
m\geq 1
, and

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}N{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}=

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}{\displaystyle\llbracket}M[{x}_{1},\!\cdots\!,{x}_{k}\leftarrow{x}]\ \langle\!\langle\raisebox{2.15277pt}{\small$C\star U$}\!/\mbox{\small$x$}\rangle\!\rangle{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigoplus_{C_{i}\in\mathsf{PER}(C)}(\nu x)(x.\overline{\mathtt{some}};x(x^{\ell}).x(x^{!}).x.\mathtt{close};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M[\widetilde{x}\leftarrow{x}]{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}

\displaystyle x.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(C)};\overline{x}(x^{\ell}).({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x^{\ell}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}\overline{x}(x^{!}).(!x^{!}.(x_{i}).{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x.\overline{\mathtt{close}})))

\displaystyle\longrightarrow^{*}

\displaystyle\bigoplus_{C_{i}\in\mathsf{PER}(C)}(\nu x^{\ell},x^{!})({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M[\widetilde{x}\leftarrow{x}]{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x^{\ell}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}!x^{!}.(x_{i}).{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigoplus_{C_{i}\in\mathsf{PER}(C)}(\nu x^{\ell},x^{!})(x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{some}}.\overline{x^{\ell}}(y_{1}).(y_{1}.\mathtt{some}_{\emptyset};y_{1}.\mathtt{close};0\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};

\displaystyle x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{u,(\mathsf{lfv}(M)\setminus\widetilde{x})};x^{\ell}({x}_{1}).\cdots x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{some}}.\overline{x^{\ell}}(y_{k}).(y_{k}.\mathtt{some}_{\emptyset};y_{k}.\mathtt{close};0\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};

\displaystyle x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{u,(\mathsf{lfv}(M)\setminus{x}_{k})};x^{\ell}({x}_{k}).x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};\overline{x^{\ell}}(y_{k+1}).(y_{k+1}.\mathtt{some}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(M)};y_{k+1}.\mathtt{close};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}

\displaystyle\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{none}}))\cdots)\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(C)};x^{\ell}(y_{1}).x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{y_{1},\mathsf{lfv}(C)};x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};\overline{x^{\ell}}({x}_{1}).({x}_{1}.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(C_{i}(1))};

\displaystyle\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}{\displaystyle\llbracket}C_{i}(1){\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{{x}_{1}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}y_{1}.\overline{\mathtt{none}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}\cdots x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(C_{i}(l))};x^{\ell}(y_{l}).x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{y_{l},\mathsf{lfv}(C_{i}(l))};x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};\overline{x^{\ell}}({x}_{l}).

\displaystyle({x}_{l}.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(C_{i}(l))};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C_{i}(l){\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{{x}_{l}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}y_{l}.\overline{\mathtt{none}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{\emptyset};x^{\ell}(y_{l+1}).(y_{l+1}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};y_{l+1}.\overline{\mathtt{close}}

\displaystyle\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{\emptyset};x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{none}})))\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}!x^{!}.(x_{i}).{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}})\qquad(:=P_{\mathbb{N}})

\displaystyle\longrightarrow^{*}

\displaystyle\bigoplus_{C_{i}\in\mathsf{PER}(C)}(\nu x^{\ell},x^{!},y_{1},{x}_{1},\cdots y_{l},{x}_{l})(y_{1}.\mathtt{some}_{\emptyset};y_{1}.\mathtt{close};0\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}\cdots\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}y_{l}.\mathtt{some}_{\emptyset};y_{l}.\mathtt{close};0

\displaystyle{\displaystyle x}.\overline{\mathtt{some}}.\overline{x^{\ell}}(y_{l+1}).(y_{l+1}.\mathtt{some}_{\emptyset};y_{l+1}.\mathtt{close};0\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{u,(\mathsf{lfv}(M)\setminus{x}_{l+1},\cdots,{x}_{k})};

\displaystyle x^{\ell}({x}_{l+1}).\cdots x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{some}}.\overline{x^{\ell}}(y_{k}).(y_{k}.\mathtt{some}_{\emptyset};y_{k}.\mathtt{close};0\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{u,(\mathsf{lfv}(M)\setminus{x}_{k})};

\displaystyle x^{\ell}({x}_{k}).x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};\overline{x^{\ell}}(y_{k+1}).(y_{k+1}.\mathtt{some}_{u,\mathsf{lfv}(M)};y_{k+1}.\mathtt{close};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}M{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{none}}))\cdots)\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}

\displaystyle{\displaystyle x}_{1}.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(C_{i}(1))};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C_{i}(1){\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{{x}_{1}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}\cdots\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}{x}_{l}.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(C_{i}(l))};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C_{i}(l){\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{{x}_{l}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}y_{1}.\overline{\mathtt{none}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}\cdots\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}y_{l}.\overline{\mathtt{none}}

\displaystyle x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{\emptyset};x^{\ell}(y_{l+1}).(y_{l+1}.\overline{\mathtt{some}};y_{l+1}.\overline{\mathtt{close}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}x^{\ell}.\mathtt{some}_{\emptyset};x^{\ell}.\overline{\mathtt{none}})\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}!x^{!}.(x_{i}).{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}})

\displaystyle\longrightarrow

\displaystyle\bigoplus_{C_{i}\in\mathsf{PER}(C)}(\nu x^{!},{x}_{1},\cdots{x}_{l})(u.\overline{\mathtt{none}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}{x}_{1}.\overline{\mathtt{none}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}\cdots\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}{x}_{l}.\overline{\mathtt{none}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}(\mathsf{lfv}(M)\setminus{x}_{1},\cdots,{x}_{k}).

\displaystyle\overline{\mathtt{none}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}{x}_{1}.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(C_{i}(1))};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C_{i}(1){\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{{x}_{1}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}\cdots\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}{x}_{l}.\mathtt{some}_{\mathsf{lfv}(C_{i}(l))};{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}C_{i}(l){\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{{x}_{l}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}!x^{!}.(x_{i}).{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}})

\displaystyle\longrightarrow^{*}

\displaystyle\bigoplus_{C_{i}\in\mathsf{PER}(C)}(\nu x^{!})(u.\overline{\mathtt{none}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}(\mathsf{lfv}(M)\setminus{x}_{1},\cdots,{x}_{k}).\overline{\mathtt{none}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}!x^{!}.(x_{i}).{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{i}})

\displaystyle\equiv\bigoplus_{C_{i}\in\mathsf{PER}(C)}u.\overline{\mathtt{none}}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}(\mathsf{lfv}(M)\setminus{x}_{1},\cdots,{x}_{k}).\overline{\mathtt{none}}={\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}\mathbb{M}{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{u}

and the result follows.

7.

Case
\mathtt{[RS{:}Fail^{!}]}
:

\displaystyle\longrightarrow^{*}(\nu x^{!})(\bigoplus_{i\in I}(\nu\widetilde{y})({\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}{x}[i]{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{j}\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}P_{i})\mathord{\;\mathbf{|}\;}!x^{!}.(x_{k}).{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\llbracket}U{\color[rgb]{0,0.5,1}\rrbracket}_{x_{k}})\qquad(*)
